- **autopilot-release**: [Zensu] Free a git working tree that a durable Autopilot run is still holding after its owner session is gone. A run is terminal only at DONE or CANCELLED, and every ordinary event — cancellation included — requires the owning session, so a run abandoned in BLOCKED or mid-stage keeps refusing every new Autopilot run in that working tree with no way for the current session to end it. This skill reports the holding run and, only after the user confirms, cancels it with one audited event that bypasses the ownership check and nothing else. - **avoid-arbitrary-stateful-objects**: Prohibits ad-hoc mutable stateful objects and enforces stateless functions or established patterns (Factory/Builder) when state is unavoidable. Use when designing data structures or reviewing code that introduces new class instances or mutable singletons. Do not use to prohibit all state — only uncontrolled, arbitrarily created mutable objects. - **avoid-arbitrary-values-in-tailwind-classes**: Prohibits Tailwind arbitrary values (e.g. w-[17px]) to enforce design system token consistency. Use when writing or reviewing Tailwind class lists. Do not use when a value is intentionally one-off and cannot be expressed by any existing token (e.g. a pixel-perfect third-party embed constraint). ([mew-ton/coding-guidelines](https://github.com/mew-ton/coding-guidelines/tree/HEAD/skills/avoid-arbitrary-values-in-tailwind-classes))
- **avoid-component-rendering-tests**: Minimizes reliance on full component mount tests by extracting testable logic and mocking child components when rendering is necessary. ([mew-ton/coding-guidelines](https://github.com/mew-ton/coding-guidelines/tree/HEAD/skills/avoid-component-rendering-tests))

- **avoid-global-state-and-init**: Prohibits package-level mutable variables and init() functions for state management in Go, enforcing dependency injection instead. - **avoid-n-1-queries**: Prevents N+1 query problems by enforcing eager loading (JOINs) or query batching when fetching related data. - **avoid-unnecessary-features-yagni**: Enforces YAGNI by prohibiting speculative features, abstractions, or configurations that are not required by a current task. - **avoid-unrequested-scope-expansion**: Prevents scope creep by keeping implementations strictly focused on the requested task without adding unrequested features or refactors. - **avoid-using-apply**: Prohibits the @apply directive in CSS files to preserve Tailwind's utility-first architecture and prevent abstraction leakage.
